TL;DR: ReCache是一个针对智能体语言模型的缓存框架，通过资源级注意力将可复用的工具和技能模式从请求中分离，实现组合不变的KV块缓存，减少了92.43%的KV张量内存并提升了3.655倍的首次令牌生成速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体语言模型在多次请求中反复以不同组合和顺序编码工具/技能模式，导致标准前缀缓存无法复用KV状态，从而产生高昂的推理计算和内存开销。

Method: （1）资源级注意力：将请求分解为独立的资源表示，消除跨资源交互并为每个资源分配局部位置，生成组合不变的KV块；（2）基于贡献的路由选择：限制资源可见性到选定的层-键值头组路由；（3）结构化与语义剪枝：仅保留调用关键字段，进一步压缩缓存。

Result: 在七个公开工具/技能使用数据集上，资源级注意力在Inv-F1上与密集调用相当（82.3%对82.4%），同时获得3.655倍的TTFT加速；完整框架将KV张量内存分配减少92.43%，注意力加速1.423倍。

Conclusion: 将可复用模式编码与选择性资源访问分离，可以大幅降低智能体推理成本且仅带来极小的效果损失，验证了该方法的有效性与通用性。

Abstract: Agentic language models repeatedly encode tool and skill schemas that recur across requests in different combinations and orders, preventing standard prefix caching from reusing their key--value (KV) states. We introduce \textbf{ReCache}, a framework for independently caching resource representations while reducing their inference-time computational and memory overhead. Resource-wise attention removes cross-resource interactions and assigns resource-local positions, producing composition-invariant KV blocks. ReCache then restricts resource visibility to contribution-selected layer--KV-head-group routes and retains only invocation-critical fields through structural and semantic pruning. We evaluate ReCache on a benchmark assembled from seven public tool- and skill-use datasets, including resource-disjoint tests. Resource-wise attention matches dense invocation performance (82.3\% versus 82.4\% Inv-F1) while providing a 3.655$\times$ time-to-first-token speedup. The complete framework reduces allocated KV-tensor memory by 92.43\% and accelerates attention by 1.423$\times$. These results show that separating reusable schema encoding from selective resource access substantially reduces agentic inference costs with limited effectiveness loss. The code is available at https://github.com/EIT-NLP/ReCache.

</details>


### [91] [The Asymmetric Harms of LLM Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19670)
*Yuan Wu,Mairui Li,Lesia Semenova,Chudi Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文系统评估了3个大语言模型在11种压缩方法下的行为变化，发现压缩对头部知识、模型置信度和社会偏见的影响呈现不对称性，而标准聚合指标（困惑度、准确率）无法捕捉这些细粒度行为偏移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型压缩虽能降低部署成本，但标准聚合指标（如困惑度和准确率）常常掩盖压缩带来的潜在行为变化，这可能导致部署后出现未预料的问题。

Method: 对3个LLM应用11种压缩方法进行系统性评估，从知识保留（头部vs尾部知识）、模型置信度、社会偏见三个维度分析压缩对模型行为的影响。

Result: 发现三个关键问题：1）压缩不成比例地降低头部知识的相对保留率（相对尾部知识）；2）压缩模型对其新丢失知识的错误答案仍保持高度自信；3）稳定的聚合偏见分数可能掩盖不同人口统计子群体间刻板偏好的大幅对立偏移。

Conclusion: 压缩会引发聚合性能指标无法捕捉的不对称行为变化，在部署前需要对压缩模型进行细粒度评估。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) compression reduces deployment costs, but standard aggregate metrics like perplexity and accuracy often mask underlying behavioral shifts. In this work, we systematically evaluate 3 LLMs across 11 compression methods to investigate the effects of compression on knowledge retention, model confidence, and social bias. We find that compression disproportionately reduces the relative retention of head knowledge compared to tail knowledge. Furthermore, compressed models often remain substantially confident in their incorrect answers on newly lost knowledge. Finally, we demonstrate that stable aggregate bias scores can conceal substantial, opposing shifts in stereotypical preferences across demographic subgroups. Together, these findings reveal asymmetric behavioral changes that aggregate performance measures fail to capture, highlighting the need for granular evaluation of compressed models before deployment.

</details>


### [92] [Projector Is All You Train](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19726)
*Nyx Iskandar,Saathvik Selvan,Slater Victoroff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文探究多模态大语言模型（MLLM）训练中是否必须微调语言模型主干。通过在3D MLLM上的实验发现，仅训练投影器（projector）即可达到与联合训练相当的多模态性能，同时避免语言模型能力的退化，且训练吞吐量约为联合训练的两倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 典型的MLLM训练过程需要同时微调语言模型主干和投影器，这带来计算成本高和语言模型能力退化等问题。作者质疑微调主干是否真的必要，希望通过仅训练投影器来简化训练流程并减少副作用。

Method: 在3D MLLM设定下进行对照实验，比较仅训练投影器与联合训练（同时微调主干和投影器）两种训练策略。使用相同的编码器和主干，在3D分类、3D描述生成以及语言、视觉和空间推理等标准基准上进行验证，并跨不同语言模型主干测试结论的泛化性。

Result: 仅训练投影器即可获得与现有基线模型及联合训练模型相当的强多模态性能；联合训练会导致语言模型既有能力的漂移/退化，而仅训练投影器可天然避免此问题；仅训练投影器的训练样本吞吐量约为联合训练的两倍。结果在不同语言模型主干上均得到验证。

Conclusion: 对于3D MLLM而言，微调语言模型主干并非适应新模态的必要条件。仅训练投影器是一种高效且稳健的替代方案，既能保持多模态性能，又能避免能力漂移并提升训练效率。

Abstract: The typical training process of a multimodal large language model (MLLM) involves adapting both the language model backbone and the projector between the backbone and a modality-specific encoder. We ask whether fine-tuning the backbone of an MLLM is necessary to adapt it to a new modality. Through experiments on 3D MLLMs, we find that training only the projector is sufficient to achieve strong multimodal performance relative to existing baseline models and our jointly trained MLLMs with the same encoder and backbone. We also show that joint training leads to undesirable drift in existing capabilities of the language model, which projector-only training avoids by definition. Furthermore, projector-only training has approximately twice the training sample throughput of joint training. We validate our findings across different language model backbones via 3D classification and captioning benchmarks as well as standard benchmarks evaluating language, vision, and spatial reasoning capabilities.

</details>


### [93] [One Success Isn't Reliability: Thinkingbox, a Sandbox and Benchmark for Agents in Stateful Business Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19741)
*Zhuochun Li,Youngmin Ko,Ali Keramati,Nicola Ferri,Susana Palmaz Lopez Pelaez,Liang-Chun Tsai,Calvin Wang,Mirco Milletari,Tuhin Kundu,Vadim Smolyakov,Kjartan Olafsson,Tommy Guy*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Thinkingbox，一个支持MCP兼容工具会话的隔离沙箱环境，并基于此构建了Thinkingbox-bench基准，包含507个跨零售、酒店、汽车保险、新银行IT和咨询IT/HR等场景的策略条件化工作流。评估发现最强模型pass@1仅65.36%，pass^20仅25.25%，且大量失败尝试显示出干净终止和合法的状态改变动作，表明响应级或工具调用级信号不能代理端到端任务完成度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有agent基准大多在可执行环境中评估（如代码修复、网页导航、API调用等），但超出代码范围的实际业务工作不仅需要产生合理响应或有效工具调用，还需要代理在多轮交互中收集缺失信息、遵循领域策略、协调依赖工具、实现正确的持久状态转换且无附带副作用。当前基准缺乏对这种状态化、策略约束的多步骤业务工作流的评估能力。

Method: 构建Thinkingbox沙箱，提供隔离的MCP兼容工具会话、完整执行轨迹和基于终端后端状态的结果评估；基于此构建Thinkingbox-bench，包含507个覆盖零售、酒店、汽车保险、新银行内部IT、咨询IT/HR等场景的策略条件化工作流，每项任务通过任务特定的可执行检查进行评估，接受有效轨迹但拒绝错误、缺失或多余的效果；部分任务额外检查最终响应的必要属性。

Result: 在闭源和开源模型中，最强模型pass@1为65.36%，但pass^20仅25.25%。许多失败试次表现出干净终止和合法的状态改变动作，说明响应或工具调用层面的信号不能作为端到端任务完成的可靠代理。Thinkingbox-bench揭示了偶尔找到成功轨迹与可靠完成状态化业务任务之间存在巨大差距。

Conclusion: Thinkingbox和Thinkingbox-bench揭示了当前agent在状态化、策略约束的业务工作流中可靠完成任务的显著不足，为未来agent评估与改进提供了一个可复现、可扩展的基准平台。基准已开源。

Abstract: Recent agent benchmarks increasingly ground evaluation in executable environments, from code repair to web navigation, app APIs, and function calling. Yet completing consequential work beyond code requires more than producing a plausible response or valid tool call: agents must gather missing information over multiple turns, follow domain policies, coordinate dependent tools, and realize the correct persistent state transition without collateral effects. In this paper, we introduce Thinkingbox, a sandbox for tool-agent-user interaction that provides isolated MCP-compatible tool sessions, complete execution traces, and outcome evaluation over terminal backend state. Built on this sandbox, Thinkingbox-bench contains 507 policy-conditioned workflows across numerous scenarios, including retail, hospitality, auto insurance, neobank internal IT, and consulting IT/HR support. Each attempt is evaluated by task-specific executable checks that accept valid trajectories while rejecting wrong, missing, or extra effects; designated tasks additionally check required properties of the final response. Across proprietary and open-weight models, the strongest achieves 65.36% pass@1, but only 25.25% pass^20. Moreover, many failed trials show clean termination and valid state-changing actions, showing that response or tool-call-level signals are not clear proxies for end-to-end task completion. Thinkingbox-bench reveals a large gap between occasionally finding a successful trajectory and reliably completing stateful business tasks. We release both Thinkingbox and Thinkingbox-Bench: https://github.com/microsoft/thinkingbox

</details>


### [94] [PersonalBench: Measuring the Authorship Gap in LLM Personalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19746)
*Yash Ganpat Sawant*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了PersonalBench基准，通过LUAR作者验证模型、LLM-as-judge和自动文体测量三个独立视角评估推理时个性化方法。研究发现现有个性化方法虽能产生作者区分度的输出（LUAR AUC=0.918），但从未跨越人类与LLM的边界，生成的文本与任何真实作者的相似度均低于人类作者之间的基线水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有个性化文本生成基准主要衡量任务准确率或偏好对齐，而非模型输出是否真正模仿目标作者的写作风格。缺乏一个能够直接评估输出与目标作者写作相似度的、经过校准的基准工具。

Method: 提出PersonalBench基准，采用三个独立评估视角：LUAR（训练的作者验证模型）、LLM-as-judge和自动化文体测量。涵盖50位作者、1000个生成样本，涉及Qwen 3和GLM-4两类模型家族，并验证了LUAR在语料中的可靠性（AUC=0.76单帖、0.96多帖）。

Result: 个性化方法能产生作者区分的输出（LUAR区分目标作者的AUC=0.918），但相似度仅为0.484-0.508，远低于人类跨作者下限0.626和上限0.756。LLM自身作者指纹占主导，生成文本与任何人类作者的距离大于随机人类之间的差异。各方法在LUAR上统计不可区分（跨度仅0.024），但LLM-judge显示表面差异，这一矛盾可追溯到特征提取与画像提取之间的循环性问题。

Conclusion: 推理时个性化方法只能调节LLM的风格，无法弥合与人类著作权的差距。PersonalBench可作为一个校准的度量工具，揭示了现有个性化方法的根本局限。

Abstract: Personalized text generation aims to make LLMs write in a specific individual's style, yet existing benchmarks measure task accuracy or preference alignment rather than whether the model's output actually resembles the target author's writing. We introduce PersonalBench, a benchmark that evaluates inference-time personalization methods through three independent lenses: LUAR (a trained authorship verification model), an LLM-as-judge, and automated stylometrics. Across 50 authors, 1,000 generations, and two model families (Qwen 3, GLM-4), we find that personalization methods do produce author-differentiated output (LUAR discriminates target authors within generated text at AUC=0.918) but this differentiation never crosses the human-LLM boundary. All methods achieve LUAR similarity to real authors in the range 0.484-0.508, below the cross-author human floor of 0.626 (ceiling 0.756). The LLM's own authorship fingerprint dominates: generated text is more distant from any human author than random humans are from each other. Methods are statistically indistinguishable from each other on LUAR (spread 0.024) despite appearing differentiated on the LLM judge, a discrepancy we trace to circularity between trait extraction and profile extraction. We validate that LUAR reliably measures authorship in our corpus (AUC=0.76 single-post, 0.96 multi-post). We release PersonalBench as a calibrated measuring stick: inference-time personalization modulates the LLM's style but does not bridge the gap to human authorship.

</details>


### [95] [FlashPrefill V2: Block-Sparse Prefill Attention for Long-Context LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19758)
*Qihang Fan,Huaibo Huang,Zhiying Wu,Bingning Wang,Ran He*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: FlashPrefill V2通过三项关键改进（均值校正项抑制近似误差、与FA3/4对齐的稀疏注意力算子重设计、原生支持分页KV缓存和连续批处理），将FlashPrefill从算法原型推进到生产级部署，在NVIDIA H20 GPU上实现最高47.26倍加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文建模是大语言模型的关键能力，但注意力机制的二次复杂度在预填充阶段构成严重瓶颈。前作FlashPrefill虽能缓解此问题，但仍停留在算法原型阶段，距离生产部署差距显著，亟需向实际长上下文服务场景推进。

Method: 1) 引入均值校正项有效抑制近似误差，即使在极高稀疏度下也能将性能损失控制在可接受范围；2) 通过PackGQA访存、warp专门化和乒乓流水线重新设计稀疏注意力算子，与最新的FlashAttention-3/4实现完全对齐并支持FP8推理；3) 原生支持分页KV缓存和连续批处理，可作为注意力后端集成到SGLang等现代推理框架中。

Result: 在NVIDIA H20 GPU（最广泛部署的推理加速器之一）上的大量评估显示：128K上下文长度下，FP8精度相对FlashAttention-2最高达47.26倍加速，BF16精度下达27.19倍加速；FP8下相对FA3/4对齐的稠密基线仍实现30.49倍加速。

Conclusion: FlashPrefill V2成功弥合了算法原型与生产级长上下文服务之间的鸿沟，在保持高稀疏度下性能稳定性的同时，实现了与现代推理框架的无缝集成和显著加速。

Abstract: Long-context modeling is a pivotal capability for Large Language Models, yet the quadratic complexity of attention remains a critical bottleneck, particularly during the compute-intensive prefilling phase. Our previous work, FlashPrefill, mitigates this cost through instantaneous pattern discovery and max-based dynamic thresholding; however, it remains an algorithmic prototype that is still distant from production deployment. In this paper, we present FlashPrefill V2, which evolves FlashPrefill from a prototype toward practical long-context serving along three dimensions. First, we introduce a mean correction term that effectively suppresses the approximation error, keeping performance degradation manageable even at extreme sparsity levels. Second, we redesign the sparse attention operator with PackGQA memory access, warp specialization, and pingpong pipelining, fully aligning with the latest FlashAttention-3/4 implementations and supporting FP8 inference to meet practical quantization requirements. Third, FlashPrefill V2 natively supports paged KV cache and continuous batching, allowing integration as an attention backend in modern inference frameworks such as SGLang. Extensive evaluations on NVIDIA H20 GPUs---among the most widely deployed inference accelerators---demonstrate that FlashPrefill V2 delivers up to 47.26x and 27.19x speedups over FlashAttention-2 at 128K context length under FP8 and BF16 precision, respectively, and, in FP8, still achieves a 30.49x speedup against an FA3/4-aligned dense baseline.

</details>


### [96] [SWE-bench Science: Can Coding Agents Resolve Engineering Tasks in Science?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19799)
*Zhipeng Xu,Jiahao Lu,Yining Zheng,Yuxin Wang,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SWE-bench Science，一个面向科学软件工程的大规模基准测试，包含来自98个GitHub仓库、20个科学领域的119个任务，从Issue驱动、专家探索和工程集成三种范式组织任务。研究发现即使最先进的编码代理（Claude Code with Opus-5）pass@1也低于50%，并识别出四类反复出现的失败机制。通过配对消融实验表明科学知识并非始终有益：准确的信息能约束修复并提升性能，而错误的引导则可能导致锚定效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学软件已成为科学仪器本身的一部分，软件故障不仅影响程序行为，还会破坏科学结论的证据基础。然而现有编码代理评估主要关注聚合任务成功率，对代理在科学软件修复中失败的根本原因缺乏深入理解。

Method: 构建SWE-bench Science基准，包含119个任务、98个GitHub仓库和20个科学领域，按Issue驱动、专家探索和工程集成三种范式组织。对最佳代理进行系统性失败机制分析，并设计配对消融实验，去除显式科学指导但保留仓库和可执行工程上下文，以评估科学知识对修复效果的影响。

Result: 最佳代理Claude Code with Opus-5在pass@1上低于50%。识别出四类失败机制：科学知识或抽象能力不足、误导性探索或表面修复、修复覆盖不完整或系统集成失败、无法将科学知识泛化到观察案例之外。消融实验表明科学知识效果不均匀：准确信息可提升平均性能和token效率，而错误引导可导致锚定效应且不必然提高精确修复成功率。

Conclusion: SWE-bench Science为研究编码代理在科学软件工程中的能力和失败机制提供了广泛的测试平台，揭示了科学知识在自动化软件修复中的双刃剑效应。

Abstract: Software increasingly functions as part of the scientific instrument itself, making failures in scientific code capable of compromising not only program behavior but also the evidence underlying scientific conclusions. Yet existing evaluations of coding agents largely emphasize aggregate task success, providing limited insight into why agents fail when repairing scientific software. We introduce \textbf{SWE-bench Science}, a repository-level benchmark for scientific software engineering comprising 119 tasks from 98 GitHub repositories across 20 scientific domains. Each task is organized into one of three paradigms: Issue-driven, Expert-exploratory, and Engineering-integration. Even the best-performing agent, \textbf{Claude Code with Opus-5 (max), achieves a pass@1 below 50\%}, highlighting the substantial challenges posed by scientific software engineering. We identify four recurring failure mechanisms: deficits in scientific knowledge or abstraction, misguided exploration or surface-level repair, incomplete repair coverage or system integration, and failures to generalize scientific knowledge beyond observed cases in our analysis. We further conduct a paired ablation that removes explicit scientific guidance while preserving the repository and executable engineering context. The results show that scientific knowledge is not uniformly beneficial: well-grounded information can constrain repair and improve average performance and token efficiency, whereas poorly aligned guidance can induce anchoring and does not necessarily improve exact repair success. Together, SWE-bench Science provides a broad testbed for studying both the capabilities and failure mechanisms of coding agents in scientific software engineering.

</details>


### [97] [LoRA-GA$^2$: Low Rank Adaptation with Multi-step Gradient Adaptive Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19800)
*Haonan He,Xinyue Fan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: LoRA-GA²是一种利用多步梯度信息的LoRA高效微调算法，通过轻量级探针捕获预训练权重的多步梯度动态，并结合频谱感知的重要性秩分配与最优初始化，在GLUE、GSM8K和HumanEval等基准上显著超越现有LoRA变体，同时保持原版LoRA的效率优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA作为一种高效微调方法在内存开销上具有优势，但与全量微调之间仍存在持续的性能差距。现有研究通过一步梯度近似来对齐LoRA更新与全量微调更新的主方向或本征维度，然而这些方法无法捕获梯度的完整动态信息，导致性能上仍有不足。

Method: LoRA-GA²提出了两个核心创新：(1) 引入一个轻量级探针来获取预训练权重的多步梯度信息，该探针不增加额外GPU内存开销，仅产生边际时间成本；(2) 基于多步梯度设计频谱感知的、基于重要性的秩分配策略和最优初始化方案，从而更充分地利用梯度的完整动态信息来指导LoRA更新。

Result: 大量实验表明LoRA-GA²在多个基准上持续优于现有LoRA变体，同时保持原版LoRA的效率优势。具体而言，在GLUE基准上比领先基线平均高出0.66分，在GSM8K上比最强基线高出1.03分，在HumanEval上高出0.87分。

Conclusion: LoRA-GA²通过充分利用多步梯度信息，有效缩小了LoRA与全量微调之间的性能差距，为基于梯度近似指导的LoRA变体提供了更强的性能-效率权衡方案。

Abstract: Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) is a prominent fine-tuning method for large models, achieving competitive performance with reduced memory overhead. However, a persistent performance gap remains between LoRA and full fine-tuning. Recent studies have sought to narrow this gap by employing one-step gradient approximations of pretrained weights to align LoRA updates with the principal directions or intrinsic dimensionalities of full fine-tuning updates. Nevertheless, these approaches fail to capture the full dynamics of the gradients. In this paper, we propose LoRA-GA$^2$, an effective fine-tuning algorithm that fully leverages multi-step gradient information. Specifically, we introduce a lightweight probe for multi-step gradients of pretrained weights that incurs no additional GPU memory cost and only marginal time overhead. We further employ a spectrum-aware, importance-based rank allocation and optimal initialization derived from multi-step gradients. Extensive experimental results demonstrate that LoRA-GA$^2$ consistently outperforms existing LoRA variants while preserving the efficiency advantages of vanilla LoRA. For instance, LoRA-GA$^2$ surpasses the leading baseline by an average of 0.66 points on the GLUE benchmark, and outperforms the strongest baseline by 1.03 points on GSM8K and 0.87 points on HumanEval, respectively.

</details>


### [98] [Stopping and Routing LLM Judge Panels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19802)
*Bin Zhu,Yi Xie,Yanghui Rao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种法官小组设计方法，将LLM评估流程中的候选法官选择形式化为角色条件分配问题，通过小型标注审计集和声明的切片信息估计法官的角色（副本、互补者、专家），从而制定调用策略：丢弃副本、全局添加互补者、按切片路由专家，并在验证增益低于阈值时停止。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估管线通常有大量候选法官（如通用LLM评判提示、奖励模型、安全分类器、置信度变体和任务专用验证器）。部署时不仅需要知道哪个法官最好，还需要决定：应调用哪些法官、在哪些样本上调用，以及何时停止构建法官小组。这一问题未被系统性解决。

Method: 将法官小组设计形式化为角色条件分配问题。从小型标注审计集、声明的数据切片和法官成本出发，估计目标相对角色：副本（不增加条件信息）、互补者（改善全局面板）、专家（仅对特定切片有效）。这些角色诱导出策略：丢弃副本、全局添加互补者、按切片条件路由专家、当验证增益低于阈值时停止构建。

Result: 在推理、代码、安全、偏好、奖励模型、摘要和数学等多个审计场景中，与单一法官、扁平面板、匹配多样性启发式、全调用堆叠、可靠性陪审团和节俭级联等方法进行了比较，生成了法官调用的机制图谱：在可部署切片上路由专家，在饱和验证器机制中停止，在风险收益值得时保留广泛集成，忽略条件副本。

Conclusion: 输出是可复用的、可审计的调用计划，可直接用于下一批评估。该方法为法官面板构建提供了系统化的决策框架，将角色分类转化为可操作的部署策略。

Abstract: LLM evaluation pipelines often have many candidate judges: general LLM-as-a-judge prompts, reward models, safety classifiers, confidence variants, and task-specific verifiers. The deployment question is not only which judge is best, but which judges should be called, on which examples, and when panel construction should stop. We formulate judge-panel design as a role-conditioned allocation problem. From a small labeled audit set, declared slices, and judge costs, the method estimates target-relative roles: copies add no conditional information, complements improve the global panel, and specialists help only on slices. These roles induce a policy: drop copies, add complements globally, route specialists conditionally, and stop when validation gain falls below a threshold. Across reasoning, code, safety, preference, reward-model, summarization, and math audits, the method is compared with single judges, flat panels, matched diversity heuristics, full-call stacking, reliability juries, and frugal cascades. The result is a regime map for judge calls: route specialists on deployable slices, stop in saturated verifier regimes, keep broad ensembles when their risk benefit is worth the cost, and ignore conditional copies. The output is a reusable, auditable call plan for the next evaluation batch.

</details>


### [99] [A knowledge-guided agentic framework for mitigating patient-context ambiguity in health queries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19875)
*Mahyar Abbasian,Saba A. Farahani,Arshia Ilaty,Hung Cao,Ramesh Jain,Amir M. Rahmani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于知识图谱的智能体框架（knowledge-guided agentic framework），用于在医疗对话机器人中缓解患者语境歧义。该框架在患者与下游语言模型之间运行，通过解释初始查询、利用任务特定知识图谱构建可行假设、识别缺失的患者语境变量并提出针对性追问，最终将原始查询与获取到的语境信息组合成澄清提示词送给下游模型。在五个语言模型、两个基准（诊断检索和膳食安全分类）上的评估显示，该框架显著优于直接回答和重复表述的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 医疗聊天机器人常收到简短、信息不足的查询，这些查询虽然语言上清晰，但可能因症状、诊断、用药、过敏或饮食限制等未披露因素支持多个合理答案。语言模型直接回答这类查询时可能依赖无依据的假设，导致错误或危险的回答。因此，需要在最终回复生成前主动缓解患者语境歧义。

Method: 提出一个知识引导的智能体框架，运行在患者和下游语言模型之间。框架流程包括：1）解释初始查询；2）使用任务特定知识图谱构建一组可行假设；3）识别区分这些假设所需的缺失患者语境变量；4）向患者提出针对性追问；5）将原始查询和获取的语境合并为澄清提示词，输入下游模型生成最终回复。框架不改变下游模型的内部结构。

Result: 在诊断检索基准（1,034个症状查询，系统性地掩盖临床相关信息）上，相比直接提示，该框架在五个评估模型上的总体精确Top-1准确率至少提升57.1个百分点，选择性精确Recall@5至少提升77.7个百分点。在膳食安全分类基准（487个查询，省略决定性健康语境）上，框架在所有五个模型上均提升了准确率，并在四个模型上取得最高Matthews相关系数。

Conclusion: 知识引导的智能体框架能有效通过主动追问获取缺失的患者信息来缓解语境歧义，显著提升下游语言模型在医疗场景中的回答准确性，且具有模型无关性（可在多个语言模型上部署），为医疗聊天机器人的安全性提供了实用改进方案。

Abstract: Patients often submit short, underspecified queries to healthcare chatbots that lack the patient-specific information needed to determine an appropriate response. Although these queries may be linguistically clear, they can support multiple plausible answers depending on undisclosed factors such as symptoms, diagnoses, medications, allergies, or dietary restrictions. A language model answering such a query directly may therefore rely on unsupported assumptions about the patient. We introduce a knowledge-guided agentic framework for mitigating patient-context ambiguity before final response generation. The framework operates between the patient and an otherwise unchanged downstream language model. It interprets the initial query, uses a task-specific knowledge graph to construct a set of plausible hypotheses, identifies the missing patient-context variables needed to distinguish among them, and asks targeted follow-up questions. The original query and the acquired context are then combined into a clarified prompt for the downstream model. We evaluated the framework across five language models using two controlled ambiguity-mitigation benchmarks: diagnosis retrieval from 1,034 symptom queries with clinically relevant evidence systematically masked, and dietary-safety classification from 487 queries with decisive health context omitted. The framework was compared with direct answering of the underspecified query and with rephrasing the same query without acquiring new patient information. In diagnosis retrieval, it increased overall exact Top-1 accuracy by at least 57.1 percentage points and selective exact Recall@5 by at least 77.7 percentage points across the five evaluated models compared with direct prompting. In dietary-safety classification, it improved accuracy across all five models and achieved the highest Matthews correlation coefficient for four...

</details>


### [100] [Interrupting the Loop: Periodic Subject Changes Raise Judged Surprise and Connection in Base Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19893)
*Roberto I. Ono Filho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究拆解了一种认知启发的生成循环，在三个基础模型上通过24种条件验证，发现大部分效果源于单一操作：在新主题注入（中断）与字面重复衰减（习惯化）共同作用时，窗口评审将惊喜度提高1.2-1.4分，联结度提高0.8分。但局部增益无法组合成完整文档，且评审无法识别实验者注入的句子、固定轮转导致模型回放早期段落等问题。该工作报告了长生成评估协议和简单干预的受控表征，而非创造力机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索基础语言模型在无任务条件下产生的"新颖性"来源，并考察长文本流中LLM评审者实际能观察到的信息。通过拆解认知启发的生成循环，识别哪些操作真正贡献了效果，以及评审协议的局限性。

Method: 在三个基础模型上运行24种条件的生成循环实验，以新主题注入（中断）和重复衰减（习惯化）为核心干预，采用窗口化评审协议（以段为单位n=10），用可重复性校验的评审器与第二评审器家族及人类读者对比。包含预注册复制实验，并测试多种变体（连接词、段落中断、上下文重置、评审门控Review等）。

Result: 中断机制比习惯化单独作用提高评审惊喜度1.2-1.4分和联结度0.8分；要求连续性的连接词有害；裸段落中断无效果；上下文重置至少与保留相当。评审无法识别注入句子（归因于模型自身）；固定轮转注入使模型回放早期段落（65-80%的窗口在150-300周期内被评审为惊喜和联结）；局部增益无法组合为完整文档。在在线装箱验证问题上，中断使有效、不同的候选启发式方法增加3-4倍但不提升最优质量。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了长生成评估协议和简单干预的受控特征描述，但明确声明不涉及创造力机制。局部增益不组合、评审器有视野局限（无法识别人为注入、被回放欺骗）、验证任务上增益不转化为最优解质量提升，这些发现对长文本生成的评估方法和干预理解具有一般性价值。

Abstract: Where does the novelty a base language model produces with no task come from, and what can an LLM judge of a long stream actually see? We dismantle a cognitively inspired generation loop over 24 conditions on three base models. Most of its effect lives in one operation: a new subject injected every few hundred tokens (an interruption) into a stream whose literal repetition is damped (habituation). We judge windows of generated text only, with the premise as the unit (n=10) and a judge measured for repeatability, against a second judge family and against human readers. Under that protocol the interruption raises judged surprise by 1.2 to 1.4 points and connection by 0.8 over habituation alone. A connective that asks for continuity hurts; a bare paragraph break adds nothing detectable on fresh text; a reset context does at least as well as a kept one; and a pre-registered replication on new premises confirms the primary contrast. Three things the window judge could not see changed the first version of this study, and we think they are of general use. The judge scores the experimenter's injected sentence as the model's own. A fixed rotation of injected sentences makes the model replay its earlier segments from beyond the judge's horizon, and the judge scores the replay as surprise and connection (65-80% of post-interruption windows at periods 150-300). And the local gains do not compose: no arm produces an integrated document. The salience monitor, the in-loop judge, memory across interruptions and a judge-gated Review run with a gate that opens add nothing. On a problem with a verifier (online bin packing), the interruption multiplies valid, distinct candidate heuristics three- to fourfold without raising the quality of the best. We report an evaluation protocol for long generation and a controlled characterization of a simple intervention, not a mechanism of creativity.

</details>


### [101] [Learning how to Forget: Fine-tuning for Long-Context Sparse Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19920)
*Matthias Seeger,Zeyu Zhang,Vihang Patil,Konstantinos Benidis,Sebastian Schelter*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于稀疏注意力模型的微调方法，适用于任意KV缓存策略，在单个NVIDIA A100 GPU（40GB）等中等硬件预算下运行，使模型能够与缓存策略协同适应，通常优于使用精确注意力（序列并行）训练的模型。作者还提供了H2O稀疏注意力的高效实现（带专用SDPA内核支持），并开源了KeysAndValues库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文推理对Transformer语言模型而言硬件开销巨大，现有工作通过稀疏注意力进行KV缓存选择和压缩来缓解这一问题，但缺乏一种能够让模型与KV缓存策略协同适应的微调方法，且现有方法通常硬件需求过高。

Method: 提出一种通用的稀疏注意力模型微调方法，可与任意KV缓存策略兼容；在中等硬件预算（如单张A100 40GB）上运行，使模型与缓存策略共同适应；并针对H2O稀疏注意力策略提供了带专用缩放点积注意力（SDPA）内核的高效实现，同时开源KeysAndValues库。

Result: 该方法在中等硬件预算下即可完成训练，且通过模型与KV缓存策略的协同适应，往往优于使用精确注意力（序列并行）训练的模型。

Conclusion: 提出并开源了KeysAndValues库，提供易用且高性能的代码，支持长上下文推理与微调中讨论的所有方法，为稀疏注意力微调提供了实用且高效的解决方案。

Abstract: A lot of prior work addressed key-value (KV) cache selection and compression by sparse attention to enable long-context inference for transformer language models without excessive hardware budgets. We provide a new method for fine-tuning models with sparse attention. It works for any KV cache policy, runs on a moderate hardware budget (e.g., a single Nvidia A100 GPU with 40 GB RAM), and allows the model to co-adapt with the policy, often outperforming models trained with exact attention (sequence parallelism). We also provide an efficient implementation of H2O sparse attention (the leading policy in our experiments) with dedicated scaled dot product attention kernel support. KeysAndValues (https://github.com/awslabs/keys_values), a new open source library for long-context inference and fine-tuning, provides easy-to-use and performant code for all methods discussed here.

</details>


### [102] [Dynamic Gated Cross-Modal Fusion with Sarcastic-aware Contrastive Regularization for Multimodal Sarcasm Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19942)
*Hao Guo,Subin Huang,Junjie Chen,Zhifa Geng,Sanmin Liu,Chao Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合动态门控跨模态融合与讽刺感知对比正则化（SaCR）的多模态讽刺检测框架，通过实例级模态贡献自适应校准和标签感知的语义一致性抑制，在MMSD和MMSD2.0数据集上超越强基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态讽刺检测中，现有方法依赖固定融合策略并将讽刺视为通用跨模态不匹配，忽略了实例依赖的模态贡献差异以及表面语义一致下隐藏的矛盾意图，导致难以捕捉细微讽刺线索和实例特定的模态交互。

Method: 提出一个多模态讽刺检测（MSD）框架，包含三个核心组件：(1) 双向门控交互模块，对跨模态特征进行过滤并在实例级别自适应校准文本与视觉的贡献；(2) 动态融合门，平衡模态重要性以生成更稳健的多模态表示；(3) SaCR标签感知对比正则化目标，在非讽刺样本上鼓励语义一致性，同时在讽刺样本上抑制误导性一致性。整体采用多目标学习策略，联合优化多模态分类与辅助单模态监督，实现端到端训练。

Result: 在MMSD和MMSD2.0数据集上的大量实验表明，所提出的方法在性能上持续优于强基线方法。

Conclusion: 动态门控融合与讽刺感知对比正则化能够有效捕捉实例特定的模态交互并纠正误导性语义一致性，从而提升多模态讽刺检测的准确性。

Abstract: Multimodal sarcasm detection aims to identify sarcastic intent from multimodal content, where inconsistencies between literal meaning and contextual cues often signal irony. This task has attracted increasing research attention. However, accurate detection remains challenging due to instance-dependent modality contributions and misleading semantic consistency, where surface-level alignment masks underlying contradictory intent. Existing methods often rely on fixed fusion strategies and treat sarcasm as generic cross-modal mismatch, limiting their ability to capture subtle sarcasm cues and instance-specific modality interactions. To address these challenges, we propose a novel MSD framework that integrates Dynamic Gated Cross-Modal Fusion with Sarcastic-aware Contrastive Regularization (SaCR). Specifically, a bidirectional gated interaction module performs cross-modal feature filtering and adaptively calibrates textual and visual contributions at the instance level. A dynamic fusion gate further balances modality importance to generate more robust multimodal representations. Furthermore, SaCR is introduced as a label-aware contrastive regularization objective that encourages semantic consistency for non-sarcastic samples while suppressing misleading consistency in sarcastic cases. The proposed framework is trained end-to-end with a multi-objective learning strategy that jointly optimizes multimodal classification and auxiliary unimodal supervision. Extensive experiments on MMSD and MMSD2.0 demonstrate that the proposed method consistently outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [103] [Natural Language Code Retrieval for 1C:Enterprise: An Open Benchmark and Efficient Bi-Encoder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19957)
*Konstantin Chesnokov,Chingiz Mingazov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了1C:Enterprise生态系统中首个自然语言代码检索的全流程解决方案，包括包含3,413个真实查询-代码对的开源基准、可复现的评估框架，以及基于Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）和隐私感知分词器微调的专用双编码器模型。模型在平衡子集宏平均nDCG@10上达到0.5992，超过基线架构（0.4932）和google/embeddinggemma-300m（0.5404），并通过重叠审计验证了结果的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 1C:Enterprise生态系统结合了俄语语法和高度领域特定的术语，但该领域缺乏开放数据集和专门模型。自然语言代码检索是一个快速发展的任务，而1C生态的特殊性使得通用模型难以直接适用，需要专门的数据和模型来解决这一空白。

Method: 1) 构建包含3,413个经过PII清洗的真实查询-代码对的开源基准和可复现的评估框架；2) 从公开代码仓库中利用google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it生成784,057个合成三元组来弥补标注数据稀缺；3) 采用Matryoshka表示学习（MRL）和隐私感知分词器微调专用双编码器；4) 报告多种子集聚合指标（平衡宏平均、查询加权微平均、论坛专用结果）；5) 通过保守的精确/13-gram重叠审计验证训练与基准之间的数据重叠不影响结果。

Result: 模型在平衡宏平均nDCG@10上达到0.5992，微平均0.5044，论坛子集0.4617。相比之下，基线架构为0.4932（宏平均），google/embeddinggemma-300m为0.5404。去除重叠审计标记的样本后，宏平均为0.6011（微平均0.5010），表明重叠不解释主要结果。MRL截断至256维保留了99.9%的检索质量，同时将密集索引存储和精确相似度计算量减少三分之二。

Conclusion: 本文展示了针对低资源、领域特定生态系统的代码检索可以通过合成数据生成、专用模型微调和MRL压缩有效实现，其结果具有挑战性优势且不受训练-基准重叠影响。MRL维度截断在不损失检索质量的前提下大幅降低了存储和计算开销。

Abstract: Natural language code retrieval is a rapidly evolving task in computer science. However, the 1C:Enterprise ecosystem combines Russian syntax with highly domain-specific terminology, for which open datasets and specialized models have been virtually non-existent. We present a comprehensive pipeline for 1C code retrieval: an open benchmark of 3,413 real-world, PII-scrubbed query-code pairs, a reproducible evaluation harness, and a specialized bi-encoder. To overcome scarce labeled data, we fine-tune on 784,057 synthetic triplets generated by google/gemma-4-26B-A4B-it from public code repositories, using Matryoshka Representation Learning (MRL) and a privacy-aware tokenizer. Because the benchmark subsets differ in size, we report balanced-subset macro, query-weighted micro, and forum-only results. Our model reaches 0.5992 balanced macro nDCG@10, 0.5044 micro, and 0.4617 on forum, versus 0.4932 macro for the baseline architecture and 0.5404 for google/embeddinggemma-300m. Removing every benchmark example flagged by the conservative exact/13-gram overlap audit leaves 0.6011 balanced macro (0.5010 micro), indicating that detected train-benchmark overlap does not explain the headline result. MRL truncation to 256 dimensions preserves 99.9% of retrieval quality while reducing dense-index storage and exact similarity arithmetic by a factor of three.

</details>


### [104] [Robust Incomplete Multimodal Sentiment Analysis via Iterative Proxy Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19971)
*Zhifa Geng,Subin Huang,Hao Guo,Junjie Chen,Sanmin Liu,Chao Kong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种迭代式代理修正框架（IPC），用于解决不完整多模态情感分析中语言模态缺失或损坏的问题。该方法从非语言模态构建语言代理，并通过门控残差修正逐步细化，再根据语言可靠度评分自适应融合，同时引入阶段式潜在修正目标以稳定训练过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实中的多模态输入常不完整或被损坏，这会削弱跨模态互补性并引入误导信息。现有基于代理的方法依赖一次性代理构建来补偿退化的语言信息，但生成的代理在初始化时可能粗糙或不可靠，过早注入会导致初始错误传播，影响情感预测的准确性。

Method: (1) 从非语言模态构建语言导向的代理表示；(2) 通过门控残差修正机制在多模态上下文下迭代逐步优化该代理；(3) 根据估计的语言可靠度评分，将修正后的代理与观测到的语言表示进行自适应融合；(4) 引入阶段式潜在修正目标，利用完整的语言表示作为训练时的语义锚点来稳定代理修正轨迹。

Result: 在MOSI、MOSEI和SIMS三个基准数据集上，于多种缺失模态设置下进行了大量实验，结果表明所提出的框架持续优于竞争性基线方法，在输入不完整条件下实现了稳健的情感预测。

Conclusion: 迭代式代理修正框架有效解决了不完整多模态情感分析中代理初始化粗糙和错误传播的问题，通过渐进式修正与自适应融合实现了对不完全输入条件下的稳健情感预测。

Abstract: Multimodal sentiment analysis aims to infer affective states by integrating language, visual, and acoustic cues. However, real-world multimodal inputs are often incomplete or corrupted, which can weaken cross-modal complementarity and introduce misleading information into downstream fusion. Existing proxy-based methods for incomplete MSA commonly rely on one-shot proxy construction to compensate for degraded language information, but the generated proxy may be coarse or unreliable at initialization. Prematurely injecting such a proxy into multimodal reasoning can propagate initial errors and compromise sentiment prediction. To address this limitation, we propose an iterative proxy correction framework for robust incomplete MSA. Our method constructs a language-oriented proxy from non-language modalities and progressively refines it under multimodal context through gated residual correction. The corrected proxy is then adaptively fused with the observed language representation according to an estimated language reliability score, allowing the model to balance proxy-based compensation and trustworthy linguistic evidence. In addition, we introduce a stage-wise latent correction objective that uses the complete language representation as a training-time semantic anchor to stabilize the proxy refinement trajectory. Extensive experiments on MOSI, MOSEI, and SIMS under diverse missing-modality settings demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently outperforms competitive baselines and achieves robust sentiment prediction under incomplete inputs.

</details>


### [105] [HealMed: Multilingual Evaluation of Large Language Models in Medicine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19981)
*Yingjian Chen,Fan Gao,Sherry T. Tong,Haoyu Zhang,Aosong Feng,Kevin W. Jin,Xing Wu,Jinghui Lu,Abdul Samad,Akbar Faruqi,Cesar Caraballo,Cibele Brandão,Dhruva,Gupta,Eunji Jeon,Gabriel Madera-Santiago,Geon Lee,Hugo Toshio Itikawa,Insook Cho,Isabelli Martins,Isarar Siddique,Israr Ahmed,Jihyo Kwak,Kanyakorn Veerakanjana,Luis Guilherme Cardoso,Minjin Kim,Piyalitt Ittichaiwong,Renee Dua,Santiago Gudiño-Rosales,Xiujie Chen,Zeo Lapalus,Zixin Xu,Michihiro Yasunaga,Rex Ying,Heuiseok Lim,Jaewoo Kang,Chanjun Park,Hang Jiang,Ethan Goh,Hyunjae Kim,Edison Marrese-Taylor,Yusuke Iwasawa,Yutaka Matsuo,Qingyu Chen,Irene Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: HealMed是一个由23名医学专家历时两年构建的多语言医学评测基准，覆盖9种语言、9个数据集、3种任务格式共9,000个样本。研究发现低资源语言上模型性能下降最显著，专有模型跨语言稳定性最佳，而医学专业化本身并不能保证多语言鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的医学大语言模型评测基准大多局限于单一语言（主要是英语），缺乏专家审核的多语言评测资源。由于翻译质量直接影响跨语言评测结果，需要构建一个经医学专家严格审核和修订的多语言医学基准，以更可靠地评估模型在不同语言环境下的医学能力。

Method: HealMed基准包含9种语言各1,000个样本，来源于9个数据集，涵盖MCQA（多项选择问答）、NLI（自然语言推理）和开放式问答三种任务格式。所有翻译均由两名同时精通英语和目标语言的医学专家进行评估与修订，整个构建过程由分布在全球9个国家和地区的23名医生和医学专家历时两年完成。评测实验比较了专有模型与开源模型、通用模型与医学专用模型在不同语言上的性能表现，并分析了专家修订对评测结果的影响。

Result: 低资源语言上性能下降最为显著，但下降幅度在不同语言和模型之间差异很大。最强的专有模型跨语言表现最稳定，而许多开源模型和医学专用模型表现出更大且不一致的语言差距。医学专用化并不保证多语言鲁棒性。另外，专家的修订既可能提高也可能降低模型测得的性能，说明翻译质量会实质性影响跨语言评测结果。

Conclusion: HealMed揭示了当前医学大语言模型在多语言场景下的性能不均衡问题，指出医学领域模型的跨语言鲁棒性不能仅靠医学数据训练实现。同时，该基准强调了翻译质量在跨语言评测中的关键作用，为构建更公平、可靠的多语言医学评测体系提供了重要参考。

Abstract: We present HealMed, an expert-reviewed benchmark for multilingual evaluation of large language models in medicine. HealMed contains 1,000 examples in each of nine languages, drawn from nine datasets and covering three task formats: MCQA, NLI and open-ended QA. The benchmark was developed over two years by 23 physicians and medical experts based across nine countries and regions. Each translation was evaluated and revised by two experts fluent in English and the corresponding target language. On HealMed, performance declined most in low-resource languages, although the size of the gap varied markedly across languages and models. The strongest proprietary models were the most stable across languages, whereas many open-source and medically specialized models showed larger and less consistent gaps. Medical specialization alone did not ensure multilingual robustness. Furthermore, expert revision could either raise or lower measured performance, indicating that translation quality materially affects cross-language evaluation results.

</details>


### [106] [Auditing Cross-Lingual Fairness in Language Model Watermarking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20047)
*Alexander Nemecek,Osama Zafar,Debargha Ganguly,Vikash Singh,Vipin Chaudhary,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文指出现有LLM水印方案几乎仅在英文上评估，并提出一个包含四组件的多语言评估框架（经验校准的检测阈值、阈值无关伴随度量、三种质量评估范式、广义熵跨语言差异分解），在6种水印方案、3种开源模型、11种语言上揭示了单语言评估无法发现的跨语言失败模式，表明水印的跨语言公平性差距与语言类型学结构相关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有水印方案评估几乎只针对英文文本，使用单一检测阈值和狭窄的质量度量集。多语言部署暴露出在英文上无关紧要的评估设计选择，但这些选择会决定跨语言结论。因此需要一个能公平、全面地评估水印方案在多语言场景下表现的方法论框架。

Method: 提出一个四组件评估框架：(1) 按部署环境经验校准的检测阈值；(2) 阈值无关的伴随度量，用于区分校准失败与检测失败；(3) 三种不重叠的质量评估范式：分布性、配对语义和参考困惑度；(4) 基于广义熵分解的跨语言差异分析，按类型学语系划分。框架应用于6种水印方案、3个开源生成模型、覆盖4种文字系统和8个类型学语系的11种语言，涵盖基础模型和指令微调两种模式。

Result: 该框架揭示了单语言单范式评估无法暴露的失败模式。在检测和质量两个维度上，观察到的差异主要出现在类型学语系之间的划分上，说明水印的跨语言公平性差距本质上是语言结构属性导致的，而非特定语言的独特现象。

Conclusion: 跨语言水印评估需要采用包含多组件、多层面的综合框架，因为仅依赖单语言、单范式的评估会掩盖系统性失败模式。水印方案在跨语言部署时的公平性问题源于语言类型学特征的结构性差异，这一发现为未来水印方案的设计和评估提供了重要的方法论指导。

Abstract: Watermarking schemes for large language model output are evaluated almost exclusively on English text using each scheme's detection threshold and a narrow set of quality measurements. Multilingual deployment exposes evaluation-design choices that are inconsequential on English but determine conclusions cross-lingually. We propose an evaluation framework with four components: detection thresholds calibrated empirically per deployment context, a threshold-independent companion measurement that distinguishes calibration failures from detection failures, three disjoint quality measurement paradigms (distributional, paired-semantic, and reference-perplexity), and a generalized-entropy decomposition of cross-language disparity over a typological family partition. Applied to six watermarking schemes, three open-weight generators, eleven languages spanning four scripts and eight typological families, and both base and instruction-tuned regimes, the framework reveals failure modes that single-language single-paradigm evaluation cannot surface. Across detection and quality, observed disparity is predominantly between-family on the typological partition, indicating that cross-lingual fairness gaps in watermarking are structural to language properties rather than idiosyncratic to particular languages.

</details>


### [107] [SABET-QA: Temporal Knowledge Graph Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20083)
*Brahim Touayouch,Mirette Moawad,Dmitry Akulov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出SABET-QA框架，通过迭代多跳推理、双向实体-时间评分机制和槽位感知上下文化模块，改进时序知识图谱上的复杂多步问答性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于嵌入的方法在时序知识图谱问答（TKGQA）中采用单遍推理流水线，难以处理需要跨多步推理的复杂时间敏感查询。

Method: 提出SABET-QA框架，包含三个核心组件：双向实体-时间评分机制（bidirectional entity-temporal scoring）用于迭代细化推理状态；槽位感知上下文化模块（slot-aware contextualization）将问题语义与时序知识图谱嵌入对齐；可微工作记忆（differentiable working memory）实现渐进式假设精化；辅助时序边界作为可用时的粗粒度监督信号。

Result: 在CronQuestions、Complex-CronQuestions、MultiTQ和TimeQuestions四个基准数据集上，SABET-QA相比强基线获得一致改进，尤其在复杂多步时序查询上表现突出。

Conclusion: 多跳迭代推理结合时序感知的评分与对齐机制，能够有效解决现有单遍推理方法在复杂多步时序问答上的不足。

Abstract: Question Answering over Temporal Knowledge Graphs (TKGQA) requires reasoning over time-sensitive facts, yet existing embedding-based methods struggle with multi-step queries due to single-pass reasoning pipelines. We propose SABET-QA, a framework that iteratively refines reasoning states across multiple hops via a bidirectional entity-temporal scoring mechanism and a slot-aware contextualization module that aligns question semantics with temporal KG embeddings. A differentiable working memory enables progressive hypothesis refinement, while auxiliary temporal boundaries serve as coarse supervision when available. Experiments on CronQuestions, Complex-CronQuestions, MultiTQ, and TimeQuestions demonstrate consistent improvements over strong baselines, particularly on complex multi-step temporal queries.

</details>


### [108] [OenoBench: A Wine-Domain Benchmark for Knowledge-Grounded Evaluation of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20106)
*Nikita Khudov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了OenoBench，一个包含3,266道多项选择题的葡萄酒领域知识基准，涵盖六大支柱和四个难度等级。基于38,104条来源可溯的原子事实构建，采用LLM辅助但以可验证来源为真相的流水线生成，并用九代理审计系统评估。对16种前沿模型配置的评估显示准确率在53%-84%之间，同时揭示了推理模式增益、模型自偏好、成本-精度权衡等关键发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有领域知识基准缺乏对来源可追溯性的严格保障，且多数基准在构建过程中过度依赖LLM生成内容作为事实来源，导致评估结果受到模型幻觉和数据污染的干扰。作者希望构建一个每一条事实都能追溯到URL、每道题都经过多重审计的领域基准，从而对前沿模型在特定垂直领域（葡萄酒）的知识能力进行可靠评估。

Method: （1）通过35个经来源验证的爬虫从政府注册机构（INAO、TTB、OIV）、同行评审期刊和Wikipedia/Wikidata提取38,104条原子化、来源锚定的事实；（2）采用五种策略在五个生成器族中生成问题，LLM仅负责重构与审核已核实的事实，而非充当事实来源；（3）通过九代理审计系统对每道题进行评分，并以Cohen's κ校准人类黄金标准；（4）对16种前沿模型配置进行系统评估，分析总体准确率、推理模式增益、自偏好偏差、成本-精度关系和闭卷可解性缺口。

Result: （i）整体准确率跨度53%-84%，o3以83.6%领先；（ii）推理模式增益集中在DeepSeek R1（+6.8pp），在Claude Opus和Gemini Pro上无增益；（iii）Anthropic在其自生成问题上呈现+9pp自偏好，Google呈现-8pp反向偏好；（iv）前沿开放权重模型与专有推理模型共享成本-精度Pareto前沿；（v）所有配置在闭卷可解题目上提升约33pp，揭示仅上下文切片可规避的参数化回忆上限。语料库、审计结果、人工审阅应用和构建代码均以CC-BY-SA-4.0发布。

Conclusion: OenoBench通过严格的来源追溯和多重审计机制，为葡萄酒领域提供了一个可靠的LLM知识评估基准。研究发现模型性能差异显著，推理模式并非普遍有效，且存在模型自偏好偏差和参数化记忆上限，这些发现对领域基准设计、模型能力评估和LLM在垂直领域的应用具有重要参考价值。

Abstract: We introduce OenoBench, a wine-domain knowledge benchmark of 3,266 multiple-choice questions across six pillars (regions, grape varieties, viticulture, winemaking, producers, business) and four difficulty tiers. The corpus is built from 38,104 atomic, source-anchored facts extracted by 35 provenance-verified scrapers from government registries (INAO, TTB, OIV), peer-reviewed journals, and Wikipedia/Wikidata. Our methodological contribution is an LLM-driven pipeline in which language models reformat verified facts and audit the result, but never serve as the source of truth: every claim traces to a URL, every question is generated by one of five strategies across five generator families, and every question is scored by a nine-agent audit calibrated against a human gold sheet via Cohen's $κ$. Evaluating sixteen frontier configurations, we find: (i) overall accuracy spans 53%-84%, led by o3 at 83.6%; (ii) reasoning-mode lift concentrates in DeepSeek R1 (+6.8pp) and is absent in Claude Opus and Gemini Pro; (iii) Anthropic shows +9pp self preference on its own questions while Google shows -8pp inverse preference; (iv) frontier open-weight models share the cost-vs-accuracy Pareto frontier with proprietary reasoning models; and (v) every config gains around 33pp on closed-book solvable items, revealing a parametric-recall ceiling that only the contextual slice avoids. We release corpus, audit findings, human-review app, and construction code under CC-BY-SA-4.0.

</details>


### [109] [When Text and Numbers Disagree: Evidence Arbitration in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20116)
*Mattia Carletti,Edward Phillips,Fredrik K. Gustafsson,Patitapaban Palo,Lei Clifton,Danielle Belgrave,Xiao Gu,David A. Clifton*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在整合冲突性异构证据（文本摘要、数值时间序列、外部工具输出）时的仲裁行为，通过受控合成基准发现模型采用启发式策略（如模态偏好、时间近因优先于可靠性线索）而非理性推理，揭示了工具增强决策系统的一个失败模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型越来越多地被用于需要整合多种信息来源（文本摘要、数值观测、外部工具输出）的决策场景，而这些来源可能提供相互冲突的证据。已有工作缺少一个可控的机制来系统研究LLM如何在冲突证据间进行仲裁，特别是在可以独立操控各种证据属性的条件下。

Method: 作者构建了一个受控的合成基准，通过潜在风险轨迹同时生成数值时间序列和自然语言摘要，并构造只有单一证据源与真实标签一致的冲突场景。该设计支持独立操控模态类型、时间近因性、来源可靠性和证据出处等维度，并在多个开源指令微调模型上进行系统性评估。

Result: 实验发现：LLM的仲裁行为是系统性的而非随机；模型表现出显著的文本vs数字模态偏好；对时间近因性的遵循程度高于对显式可靠性线索的遵循；当外部预测与直接上下文证据冲突时模型会过度依赖外部预测。

Conclusion: 当前LLM在整合异构证据时常依赖启发式仲裁策略而非证据本身的可靠性，这构成工具增强型决策系统的一个重要失败模式，提示需要改进模型对证据权衡的推理能力。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in settings where textual summaries, numerical observations, and external tool outputs may provide conflicting evidence. We study how LLMs arbitrate between such sources when they support opposing decisions. To do so, we introduce a controlled synthetic benchmark in which latent risk trajectories generate both numerical time series and natural language summaries, allowing us to construct conflicts where exactly one evidence source is aligned with the ground-truth label. This design lets us independently manipulate modality, temporal recency, source reliability, and evidence provenance. Across open-weight instruction-tuned models, we find that arbitration behaviour is systematic rather than random: models exhibit distinct text-versus-number preferences, follow temporal recency more consistently than explicit reliability cues, and can over-rely on external forecasts even when they conflict with direct contextual evidence. These results suggest that current LLMs often rely on heuristic arbitration strategies when integrating heterogeneous evidence, highlighting a failure mode for tool-augmented decision systems.

</details>


### [110] [FormalTCS: Benchmarking End-to-End Frontier Formal Theoretical Computer Science Research of Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20153)
*Dingzirui Wang,Xuanliang Zhang,Keyan Xu,Qingfu Zhu,Wanxiang Che*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了\ourbenchmark，一个由专家验证的基准，用于评估LLM在前沿、端到端理论计算机科学（TCS）研究上的能力。该基准包含175个来自2025-2026年STOC、FOCS、SODA和COLT论文的实例，并带有专家验证的Lean形式化及证明。评估显示当前最优模型在自动形式化（11.5分）上远落后于直接证明人类给出的形式化陈述（28.6 Pass@8），表明自动形式化是最大瓶颈；同时，基于该基准构建的自动TCS研究框架生成的64个新主张中仅6个通过专家评估和证明验证，说明"研究品味"不足是自主研究的另一大障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用于TCS研究的基准远非真实研究场景，缺乏包含论文特定定义、假设和证明依赖的前沿、端到端研究评估，需要更贴近真实研究的基准来准确衡量模型能力并识别瓶颈。

Method: 构建\ourbenchmark作为专家验证的前沿端到端TCS研究基准：从2025-2026年STOC、FOCS、SODA、COLT接收论文中提取175个实例，保留论文特定定义、假设和证明依赖，并提供专家验证的Lean形式化及证明；在此基础上开发自动化TCS研究框架，实现新主张的生成、形式化、筛选和证明。

Result: 领先LLM评估显示：最佳模型在将自然语言主张翻译为形式化定理陈述上仅得11.5分，而在证明人类提供的正式陈述上达到28.6 Pass@8，自动形式化为最尖锐瓶颈；自动框架生成的64个新主张中仅6个通过专家评估和证明验证。

Conclusion: 当前LLM仍远未可靠完成完整的TCS研究流程，自动形式化是主要瓶颈；超越形式化之后，有限的研究品味（research taste）是自主TCS研究的另一大障碍。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown growing potential for automated theoretical computer science (TCS) research, yet existing benchmarks remain far from realistic research settings. We introduce \ourbenchmark, an expert-validated benchmark for evaluating LLMs on frontier, end-to-end TCS research. \ourbenchmark contains $175$ instances drawn from papers accepted to STOC, FOCS, SODA, and COLT in 2025-2026, preserving paper-specific definitions, assumptions, and proof dependencies, with expert-verified Lean formalizations and proofs. Evaluations of leading LLMs reveal that current models remain far from reliably completing the full research pipeline. In particular, autoformalization is the sharpest bottleneck: the best model achieves only $11.5$ on translating natural-language claims into formal theorem statements, compared with $28.6$ Pass@8 when proving human-provided formal statements. Building on \ourbenchmark, we further develop an automated TCS research framework that generates, formalizes, filters, and proves new claims. Of $64$ generated claims, only $6$ ultimately pass expert evaluation and proof verification, indicating that beyond formalization, limited research taste remains another major barrier to autonomous TCS research.

</details>


### [111] [Task-CoEvolve: Efficient Harness Optimization via Adaptive Validation Task Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20169)
*Atsuyuki Miyai,Kiyoharu Aizawa,Toshihiko Yamasaki*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Task-CoEvolve是一种通过自适应验证任务选择来优化LLM智能体harness的高效方法，利用方差加权采样聚焦于智能体能力边界附近的任务，同时通过概率校正估计全量集性能，在保持全量搜索最终性能的同时将优化过程中的评估次数减少80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的harness优化方法在每次迭代时都对固定的验证集进行完整评估，即使某些任务随着harness演化变得不再具有区分度，导致大量不必要的评估开销和计算成本浪费。

Method: Task-CoEvolve的核心方法包括：(1) 基于候选harness在不同任务上的分歧程度来判断任务的信息量，任务分歧越大越具区分价值；(2) 使用基于历史结果的方差加权采样，使采样分布聚焦于智能体能力边界附近的任务并随harness演化自适应调整；(3) 通过考虑采样概率进行加权估计，从部分评估结果中无偏估计全量集分数，保证跨迭代比较的一致性。

Result: 在在线文本分类和Terminal-Bench 2.1上的实验表明，Task-CoEvolve一致优于固定子集基线，达到与全量搜索相同的最终性能，同时将优化过程中的评估次数减少了80%。

Conclusion: 通过将验证任务与harness协同演化，可以在不牺牲最终性能的前提下大幅降低harness优化所需的评估成本，为大规模LLM智能体优化提供了高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We present a novel approach to efficient LLM agent harness optimization through adaptive validation task selection. Harness optimization iteratively rewrites the harness code based on validation performance, enabling substantial performance gains without updating the underlying model weights. Existing approaches, however, evaluate a fixed validation set in full at every iteration, incurring substantial evaluation costs even on tasks that become less discriminative as the harness evolves. We propose $\textbf{Task-CoEvolve}$, which co-evolves the validation tasks with the harness by addressing two challenges: selecting informative tasks and estimating full-set performance from partial evaluations. Task-CoEvolve builds on the observation that tasks on which candidate harnesses disagree are more informative for distinguishing among them than tasks that are consistently solved or failed. It uses variance-weighted sampling based on past outcomes to focus evaluation on tasks near the agent's capability frontier, with the sampling distribution adapting as the harness evolves. It then estimates full-set scores from the sampled tasks by accounting for their sampling probabilities, enabling consistent comparisons across iterations despite evaluating different subsets. Experiments on online text classification and Terminal-Bench 2.1 show that Task-CoEvolve consistently outperforms fixed-subset baselines and matches the final performance of full-set search while reducing the number of evaluations during optimization by 80%. Code will be released at https://github.com/Agent4Science-UTokyo/Task-CoEvolve.

</details>


### [112] [Inject, Align, Recover: Staged Post-Training for Retrieval-Free Document Knowledge Internalization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20281)
*Qian Kou,Xiaofeng Shi,Xiaosong Qiu,Hua Zhou*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出IAR（注入-对齐-恢复）三阶段后训练框架，用于将固定文档集转化为可用的参数化知识，实现免检索的文档问答。在多个模型族（Llama、Phi、Qwen、SmolLM）和数据集上，IAR相较Vanilla SFT在领域QA准确率平均提升3.6个百分点，通用性能平均提升12.1个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型在推理时若未检索到相关源文档，往往难以回答关于有界文档集的问题。现有方法（如持续预训练）未能有效将固定语料转化为参数化知识，且难以同时兼顾领域内化效果与通用能力保持。

Method: IAR包含三个阶段：(1) Inject：将源文档转换为续写、改写和指令条件重建三种目标进行结构化知识注入；(2) Align：使用仅回答的QA监督微调，适配问答行为；(3) Recover：将领域适配模型与基础指令模型合并，恢复通用能力。

Result: 在Common Corpus（CC）和CCI数据集上，跨Llama、Phi、Qwen、SmolLM四个模型族验证，IAR在7/8的数据集-模型设置中四项指标均优于Vanilla SFT。领域QA准确率平均+3.6个百分点，通用性能（IFEval、MMLU、MSBench）平均+12.1个百分点。扩展的CC基线显示LoRA和FAPM在个别通用指标上可胜出，但在同时达到领先或接近领先领域内化效果的方法中，IAR保持最强的通用能力曲线。

Conclusion: IAR通过将知识注入、行为对齐和通用能力恢复分离为三个独立阶段，有效提升了免检索文档问答的领域-通用性能前沿，为文档知识内化提供了一种优于传统持续预训练和Vanilla SFT的解决方案。

Abstract: Large language models often fail to answer questions about a bounded document collection when the source documents are not retrieved at inference time. We study this setting as document knowledge internalization: converting a fixed corpus into usable parametric knowledge for retrieval-free question answering. We propose IAR (Inject, Align, and Recover), a three-stage post-training framework that separates structured document knowledge injection, QA behavior alignment, and general ability recovery. Unlike conventional continued pretraining, Inject converts source documents into continuation, rewrite, and instruction-conditioned reconstruction objectives. Align then adapts the injected model with answer-only QA supervision, while Recover merges the domain-adapted model with the base instruction model to recover general capabilities. Across Common Corpus (CC) and CCI, and across Llama, Phi, Qwen, and SmolLM model families, IAR improves the domain-primary domain-general frontier for retrieval-free document internalization. In the main comparison, IAR improves over Vanilla SFT on all four reported metrics in 7 of 8 dataset-model settings, with average gains of 3.6 percentage points in domain QA accuracy and 12.1 percentage points in mean general performance across IFEval, MMLU, and MSBench. Extended CC baselines show that LoRA and FAPM can win individual general metrics, but among methods that also reach leading or near-leading domain internalization, IAR retains one of the strongest general profiles.

</details>


### [113] [Inducing Task Models from Computer-Use Traces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20319)
*Yucheng Jiang,Zora Zhiruo Wang,Ruishi Chen,Diyi Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出任务模型归纳（Task Model Induction, TMI）方法，从无约束的自然计算机使用轨迹中自动发现潜在任务、解耦并发活动，并为每个潜在任务归纳出结合层级目标模型与程序流程控制模型的程序化任务模型。在受控人类和智能体轨迹上，TMI 在任务分组上与真实标注达成0.974的一致性，重构74.9%的观察执行步骤，并从任务模型中提炼的技能使保留任务准确率提升30.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机使用智能体进入真实工作场景时，需要学习实际任务执行方式，而组织也需要审计和复用相关知识。自然主义轨迹数据（被动记录的屏幕截图与鼠标键盘操作）虽蕴含丰富信息，但现有方法要么假设给定任务或单一工作流，要么仅生成步骤级摘要而非结构化任务模型，无法应对真实工作中多线程交错目标的复杂性。

Method: TMI 包含两个核心步骤：（i）在无约束轨迹中发现潜在任务，解耦并发活动；以及（ii）将每个潜在任务归纳为任务模型，该模型配对两类子模型——层次化目标模型，刻画递归式的目标分解结构；以及流程模型，描述组织执行的控制流。

Result: 在受控的人类和智能体轨迹上：TMI 对交错任务的重构与真实分组的一致性达0.974；重构了74.9%的观察执行步骤，远超最强工作流归纳基线；从 TMI 任务模型中导出的技能使保留任务准确率较最强基线提升30.0%。

Conclusion: TMI 能够从自由、多线程的计算机使用轨迹中诱导出可审计、可复用的结构化任务模型，为计算机使用智能体在实际工作中学习和组织审计知识提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Naturalistic computer-use traces, passively recorded screenshots and mouse or keyboard actions, are a valuable resource for deriving symbolic, auditable, and reusable models of how everyday work is done. Such models matter as computer-use agents enter real work, where agents need to learn how tasks are actually performed, and organizations need to audit and reuse that knowledge. However, inducing such task models is challenging, as activity is observed only as low-level events and real-world work is multi-threaded with interleaved goals. Existing methods assume a given task or a single workflow, and produce step-level summaries rather than structured task models. We introduce Task Model Induction (TMI), which (i) discovers the latent tasks in an unconstrained trace, disentangling concurrent activity, and (ii) for each latent task, induces a task model pairing a hierarchical objective model of recursive goal decomposition with a procedure model of the control flow that organized the execution. Intrinsically, on controlled human and agent trajectories, TMI recovers interleaved tasks with 0.974 agreement against ground-truth groupings and reconstructs 74.9% of the observed execution steps, far more than the strongest workflow induction baseline. Extrinsically, skills derived from TMI's task models improve held-out task accuracy by 30.0% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [114] [G-CARL: Grounded Checklist-Aligned Reward Learning for Patient-Oriented Medical Report Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20331)
*Shiao Xie,Siyu Chen,Jianwei Lv,Bo Yuan,Yujin Wang,Xiandong Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了面向患者的医学报告解读（PMRI）这一新型多模态生成任务，并设计了G-CARL框架，通过多源检索验证和上下文感知的checklist加权强化学习，在事实准确性和患者沟通质量之间取得平衡。同时构建了真实世界的MMedReport基准和临床医生设计的三维评估协议，实验表明G-CARL在整体质量、细粒度精度和checklist召回率上均优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 患者对医学报告进行个性化解读的需求日益增长，这既要求基于证据的医学事实准确性，又要求基于上下文的患者沟通能力。然而现有的医学视觉-语言任务未能同时捕捉这两个核心需求，且两者在可验证性上存在根本差异却紧密耦合，在传统监督微调和整体式强化学习范式中难以联合优化。

Method: 提出G-CARL（grounded, checklist-aligned reinforcement learning）框架，包含两个关键组件：（1）多源检索用于原子声明验证，为事实性提供结构化监督；（2）上下文感知、实例特定的加权checklist，用于回应覆盖率监督，涵盖事实性、用户需求满足度和表达质量三个方面，且不限制回复多样性。此外构建了MMedReport基准和临床医生设计的三维评估协议。

Result: 实验表明G-CARL在整体质量、声明级精度和checklist召回率上持续优于现有后训练基线；临床医生的成对偏好评估进一步确认G-CARL生成的解读更准确且更符合患者需求。

Conclusion: G-CARL通过将事实验证与上下文感知的checklist监督相结合，有效解决了医学报告解读中事实性与患者沟通之间难以联合优化的问题，为医学多模态生成任务提供了新的优化范式。

Abstract: Personalized interpretation of medical reports has emerged as an increasingly important need among patients. Addressing this need requires both evidence-grounded medical factuality and context-dependent patient communication, yet existing medical vision-language tasks do not adequately capture these dual requirements. To bridge this gap, we introduce Patient-oriented Medical Report Interpretation (PMRI), a novel open-ended multimodal generation task that requires models to explain medical reports in accurate and accessible language based on a user's query and dialogue history. These two objectives differ fundamentally in their verifiability, yet remain tightly coupled, making them difficult to optimize jointly under conventional supervised fine-tuning and holistic reinforcement learning paradigms. To address this challenge, we propose G-CARL, a grounded, checklist-aligned reinforcement learning framework that combines multi-source retrieval for atomic claim verification with context-aware, instance-specific weighted checklists for response coverage, providing structured supervision for factuality, user-demand satisfaction, and expression quality without constraining response diversity. We further construct MMedReport, a real-world PMRI benchmark, along with a clinician-designed three-dimensional evaluation protocol. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G-CARL consistently outperforms existing post-training baselines in overall quality, claim-level precision, and checklist recall. Pairwise preference evaluation by clinicians further confirms that G-CARL produces interpretations that are more accurate and better aligned with patient needs.

</details>


### [115] [ConceptGuard: Benchmarking Context-Sensitive Unlearning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.20338)
*Sahil Kale,Ian Harris*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出"双用途概念"（dual-use concepts）的概念，构建了名为ConceptGuard的基准，用于在不牺牲良性知识的前提下评估LLM的概念级选择性遗忘（unlearning）能力，并揭示现有方法在此设定下表现不佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM遗忘方法和基准存在根本性缺陷：它们依赖于互不相交的遗忘集和保留集（由独立事实组成），仅用简单直接的事实回忆来评估成功与否。这种框架忽略了遗忘的关键要求——在消除有害行为的同时保留良性且有价值的知识。有效的遗忘必须在概念层面运作，确保完全移除不安全的用途同时保持正确有用的用法。

Method: 1) 提出"双用途概念"（dual-use concepts）概念：可用于有害和良性两种语境的概念。2) 基于此构建ConceptGuard基准，其中遗忘集和保留集在概念使用上明确互补。3) 基准在概念层面而非稀疏事实层面探索和衡量遗忘，且评估是对意图敏感的（intent-sensitive），以最大化语境分离为目标促进更安全的行为。4) 使用ROUGE和概念级指标评估现有遗忘技术。

Result: 现有遗忘技术在此设定下表现不佳：显示出弱的语境分离能力、较差的ROUGE和概念级指标性能。结果揭示了强遗忘-效用权衡（forgetting-utility trade-offs）、语境敏感性提升有限、以及跨方法在概念级控制上的一致性较差。

Conclusion: 当前LLM遗忘技术无法在实际概念层面实现完整的遗忘，需要开发与真实世界安全需求更对齐的遗忘方法。论文提出的ConceptGuard基准为未来的遗忘研究提供了更贴合实际的评估框架，数据集已公开。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly require selective removal of harmful or sensitive knowledge, called unlearning, yet existing methods and benchmarks fail to evaluate this capability completely. Current approaches rely on disjoint forget and retain sets composed of independent facts, and measure success using simple and direct factual recall. This framing fails to capture a key requirement of unlearning, namely the ability to eliminate harmful behaviors while preserving benign and beneficial knowledge. We argue that effective unlearning must operate at the level of concepts, ensuring complete removal of unsafe applications while maintaining their correct and useful usage, thereby achieving conceptually meaningful and complete unlearning. To better evaluate unlearning techniques from such a practical viewpoint, we introduce the notion of dual-use concepts: concepts that can be used in both harmful and benign contexts. Building on these concepts, we construct a benchmark called ConceptGuard where forget and retain sets are explicitly complementary in concept usage. Our benchmark uniquely enables unlearning to be explored and gauged at the level of concepts, instead of sparse facts, and evaluation is intent-sensitive with the goal of maximizing contextual separation to promote safer behavior. We demonstrate that current unlearning techniques perform poorly under this setting, showing weak contextual separation alongside poor performance in ROUGE and concept-level metrics. Our results reveal strong forgetting-utility trade-offs, limited gains in contextual sensitivity, and poor consistency in concept-level control across methods, and provide ideas for unlearning approaches that better align with real-world safety requirements. Our dataset is publicly available.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [116] [Active Inference as Context Acquisition for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19202)
*Sanchayan Dutta,Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出将交互式AI代理的上下文获取过程形式化为主动推理（active inference）问题：在内层推理步骤更新对潜在任务状态的信念，在外层决策步骤选择下一个上下文动作、任务动作或停止动作，以最小化带成本的期望自由能。在确定性设定下，认知项可简化为期望信息增益（可可选地按token成本归一化）。作者将该框架实例化为最优提问（OQA），使用精确后验和动态规划oracle，并在25至300个候选的二元和多分类任务上对前沿语言模型进行基准测试，同时研究了生成前澄清问题和在token预算下的自动提示优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交互式AI代理必须尽可能高效地获取正确的上下文。当用户省略约束、偏好、文件或任务变量时，代理可以选择默认假设继续执行，或花费token进行澄清提问、检索调用、工具调用或提示试验。这种权衡缺乏统一的数学形式化，本文旨在将其建模为上下文获取的主动推理问题。

Method: 建立双层推理框架：内层推理步骤更新关于潜在任务状态的信念（贝叶斯后验），外层决策步骤在上下文动作、任务动作和停止动作之间进行选择，以最小化期望自由能（含成本项）。在确定性设定中，认知项退化为期望信息增益并可按token成本归一化。实例化为OQA框架，采用精确后验计算和动态规划作为oracle求解最优策略，用于基准测试前沿语言模型。

Result: 在25到300个候选的二元和多分类任务上对前沿语言模型进行了基准测试；同时研究了澄清生成（clarification before generation）及在token预算约束下的自动提示优化场景。

Conclusion: 该公式是模型无关的，将主动推理视为AI代理上下文获取层的一种设计原则，并为代理如何权衡澄清与默认行动之间的token成本提供了理论框架。

Abstract: Interactive AI agents must acquire the right context as efficiently as possible. When a user omits a constraint, preference, file, or task variable, an agent can proceed with a default assumption or spend tokens on a clarifying question, retrieval call, tool call, or prompt trial. We formulate this tradeoff as active inference for context acquisition. An inner inference step updates beliefs over a latent task state, and an outer decision selects the next context action, task action, or stop action to minimize expected free energy under cost. In deterministic settings, the epistemic term reduces to expected information gain, optionally normalized by token cost. We instantiate the framework in Optimal Question Asking (OQA), with exact posteriors and a dynamic programming oracle, and benchmark frontier language models on binary and multiway categorical tasks from 25 to 300 candidates. We also study clarification before generation and automated prompt optimization under token budgets. The formulation is model-agnostic and views active inference as a design principle for the context-acquisition layer of AI agents.

</details>


### [117] [Robust Metaheuristics under Uncertainty for Berth Allocation and Quay Crane Assignment: A Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19214)
*Yang Li,Peilan Xu,Wenjian Luo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地综述了不确定环境下基于群体的元启发式算法在泊位分配与岸桥调度问题（BACAP）中的应用，从机制导向的角度梳理了现有方法，并提出了基准测试套件及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BACAP是港口码头调度中的代表性优化问题，但在实际执行中面临到达偏差、处理时间波动和资源中断等不确定性，名义假设下的调度方案在执行中可能变得脆弱。尽管群体元启发式算法已广泛用于BACAP，但现有研究在不确定性表示、鲁棒性准则、搜索机制和实证评估协议等方面较为分散，缺乏系统性的综述梳理。

Method: 本文采用机制导向的分类框架组织现有方法，涵盖四个维度：解表示与解码、鲁棒评估与选择、鲁棒性引导的搜索动力学、可行性保持与恢复。同时提出了一个面向不确定BACAP的基准测试套件，并通过组合代表性元启发式算法与不同鲁棒性策略报告了基准结果。

Result: 梳理了BACAP中不确定性来源与信息表示方式，从机制角度系统分类了现有鲁棒群体元启发式方法，构建了用于受控实证比较的基准套件，并给出了初步的基线结果。

Conclusion: 指出了四个开放挑战：基准扩展、鲁棒性感知的搜索设计、时间自适应鲁棒性以及非平稳不确定性处理，为未来研究指明了方向。

Abstract: The berth allocation and quay crane assignment problem (BACAP) is a representative port-terminal scheduling problem in maritime transportation and freight logistics, where vessel arrivals, berth positions, service durations, and quay?crane availability are tightly coupled. Under uncertainties such as arrival deviations, handling-time fluctuations, and resource disruptions, schedules optimized under nominal assumptions may become fragile during execution, motivating the study of robust metaheuristic optimization for BACAP in port-terminal operations. Although population-based metaheuristics have been widely used for BACAP and related port-scheduling problems, existing studies remain fragmented in their uncertainty repre?sentations, robustness criteria, search mechanisms, and empir?ical evaluation protocols. To the best of our knowledge, this paper provides the first focused review dedicated to robust population-based metaheuristics for BACAP under uncertainty. We first summarize uncertainty sources and information repre?sentations in BACAP, and then organize existing methods from a mechanism-oriented perspective, covering solution representation and decoding, robust evaluation and selection, robustness-guided search dynamics, and feasibility preservation and recovery. We further present a benchmark suite for uncertain BACAP to support controlled empirical comparison and report illustrative baseline results by combining representative metaheuristics with different robustness strategies. Finally, we identify open chal?lenges related to benchmark extension, robustness-aware search design, time-adaptive robustness, and non-stationary uncertainty.

</details>


### [118] [How to Navigate Uncertainty About AI Consciousness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19215)
*Dr Tom McClelland*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出在面对人工智能是否具有意识的深层不确定性时，应将关注焦点从不可解的AI意识问题转向可解的AI效价（valence）问题，即评估AI是否具有若其有意识则构成效价体验的状态，以此为基础建立对潜在有感知AI的负责任开发路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在人工智能可能具有意识的深层不确定性下，我们面临两难困境：一方面假设AI无意识可能对值得道德关怀的实体造成严重伤害；另一方面假设AI有意识则可能在无意识的机器上浪费资源。AI意识问题的不可解性使这一困境难以摆脱，因此需要一种新的解决路径。

Method: 作者提出将不可解的AI意识问题转化为可解的AI效价问题。具体而言，通过评估AI是否具有那些如果AI有意识就会构成效价体验的状态（如快乐或痛苦相关状态），从而绕过对意识本身的直接判断，为负责任地对待潜在有感知AI提供道德基础。

Result: 作者表明，这种以效价为核心的评估框架足以支撑对潜在有意识AI开发的负责任方法，为道德决策提供可操作的标准，而不必依赖于对意识本质的最终确定。

Conclusion: 该论文主张通过概念转换——从意识转向效价——来化解AI道德困境，使我们在不确定的条件下仍能采取负责任且可操作的行动。

Abstract: Given deep uncertainty about the possibility of artificial consciousness, it is unclear how we should treat potentially sentient AI. On the one hand, we could assume insentience but risk doing terrible harms to entities that deserve moral standing. On the other hand, we could assume sentience and instead risk wasting resources on insentient machines. The intractability of questions around AI consciousness mean that this dilemma is hard to escape. I suggest a way out of that shifts from intractable questions of AI consciousness to tractable questions of AI valence. Specifically, we can assess whether an AI has states that would constitute valenced experiences if it were conscious. I show how this is sufficient to ground a responsible approach to the development of potentially conscious AI.

</details>


### [119] [Bounded Sovereignty and the Control Tax: Pricing AI Oversight When the Deployer Does Not Own the Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19216)
*Zhen Wen Lim*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"有限主权"（bounded sovereignty）概念，分析在受监管组织中通过API或托管端点部署前沿模型时，部署者仅拥有部分技术与合同访问权限对AI控制协议可执行性的影响，并给出四层访问类型学、协议-层需求矩阵及"主权折扣成本"概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI控制研究假设部署者能对模型及周边管线进行全面仪表化，但这在受监管组织通过API或托管端点使用前沿模型时往往不成立——部署者可控制业务流程，却无法控制模型权重、服务基础设施、内部轨迹、更新流程或完整交互日志，导致许多控制协议无法实际执行。

Method: 提出四层访问类型学（数据、模型、基础设施、交互层），构建协议-层需求矩阵，引入主权折扣成本概念，并通过135万条合成案例模拟的访问消融实验（construct-validity验证）及匿名国家支付基础设施场景进行解释。

Result: 完整日志改善诊断能力；预执行网关使干预成为可能；轨迹访问与模型版本控制强化事后解释；范围限制可在提高安全性的同时降低有用性。实验本身并非真实支付系统证据，而是构念效度练习。

Conclusion: 控制协议作为通用安全方案时，应明确声明其访问假设（access assumptions）。

Abstract: AI control research asks how to deploy models safely even when they may be misaligned, but many control protocols assume that the deployer can instrument the model and its surrounding pipeline. That assumption often fails for regulated organisations using frontier models through APIs or managed endpoints, where the deployer may control the business process but not the model weights, serving infrastructure, internal traces, update process, or full interaction logs. This paper introduces bounded sovereignty: partial technical and contractual access across the data, model, infrastructure, and interaction layers of the AI stack. It argues that these access conditions determine which control protocols can be executed in practice. The paper contributes a four-layer access typology, a protocol-by-layer requirements matrix, and the concept of sovereignty discount cost: the part of the control tax spent substituting for missing access through contracts, architecture, audit, vendor assurance, residual risk, or reduced system scope. It also reports a synthetic access-ablation experiment over 1.35 million synthetic case simulations and interprets the findings through an anonymised national-payments-infrastructure scenario. The experiment is not real-world payment-system evidence; it is a construct-validity exercise. The results show that complete logs improve diagnosis, a pre-execution gateway enables intervention, trace access and model-version control strengthen post-incident explanation, and scope restriction can improve safety while reducing usefulness. Control protocols proposed as general safety solutions should therefore state their access assumptions explicitly.

</details>


### [120] [Interaction valence reveals contrasting social networks in dairy cattle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19222)
*Sibi Parivendan,Suresh Raja Neethirajan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于价值（valence）感知的社会网络框架，从商业奶牛场挤奶前区域的连续视频中提取奶牛间的亲和性与对抗性互动，构建畜群级社交网络。自动化管线在预期类别平衡审计中与人工标注的一致性达82.8%，宏观F1为0.872。聚合网络结构连通，亲和性事件形成5个算法社群；对抗性事件占保留事件72.4%。将事件按价值分离后，亲和性与对抗性网络层在边集、社群划分和个体位置上均存在显著差异，表明混合计数会掩盖网络的行为组成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自动化牲畜监测通常将行为视为孤立事件，忽略了社交关系对资源获取、冲突暴露和群体稳定性的影响。作者旨在开发一种能够将视频交互转化为畜群层面社交网络表征的框架，特别关注区分亲和性（affiliative）与对抗性（agonistic）行为，以揭示个体在群体中的社会角色与结构位置。

Method: 采用基于姿态的计算机视觉管线，对某商业奶牛场挤奶前区域连续7小时39分钟的视频进行分析。经过质量控制后，保留1,183个候选交互，涉及36头奶牛和177个二元组。采用预测类别平衡抽样审计（198个片段），通过自动标签与人工标注的一致性及宏观F1评估性能，并构建聚合网络及按价值分离的分层网络，计算密度、传递性、平均路径长度、模块度、中间中心性等网络指标。

Result: 审计样本中自动与人工标签一致性为82.8%，宏观F1为0.872。聚合网络连通（密度0.281，传递性0.513，平均路径长度1.88），亲和性事件形成5个算法社群（模块度Q=0.429）。对抗性事件占保留事件的72.4%和互动时长的76.0%。互动伙伴最多的奶牛并非中间中心性最高者。按价值分离后，亲和性与对抗性层在边集、社群划分和个体位置上呈现描述性差异。

Conclusion: 混合计数互动会掩盖观察网络的行为组成，价值感知的网络分析提供了检验竞争、亲和及福利相关变化假说的框架。但该框架在作为福利或健康指标使用之前，仍需纵向验证。

Abstract: Social relationships shape access to resources, exposure to conflict and group stability, yet automated livestock monitoring typically treats behaviour as isolated events. Here, we present a valence-aware social-network framework that transforms video-derived interactions into herd-level representations of affiliative and agonistic organization. A pose-based computer-vision pipeline analysed 7 h 39 min of continuous video from the pre-milking area of one commercial dairy farm. After quality control, 1,183 of 1,414 candidate interactions remained, involving 36 cows and 177 dyads. In a predicted-class-balanced audit of 198 pipeline-detected clips, automated and manual labels agreed in 82.8% of cases, with an unweighted audit-sample macro-F1 of 0.872. These values describe the audited sample rather than prevalence-weighted or end-to-end deployment performance. The aggregated network was connected (density = 0.281; transitivity = 0.513; mean path length = 1.88), and predicted affiliative events formed five algorithmic communities (modularity Q = 0.429). Within the observed zone, predicted agonistic interactions comprised 72.4% of retained events and 76.0% of interaction duration. The cow with the most partners did not have the highest betweenness centrality. Separating events by predicted valence produced descriptively different affiliative and agonistic layers, with contrasting edge sets, community partitions and individual positions. Thus, pooled interaction counts can obscure the behavioural composition of an observed network. Valence-aware analysis provides a framework for testing hypotheses about competition, affiliation and welfare-relevant change, while requiring longitudinal validation before use as a welfare or health indicator.

</details>


### [121] [Air Traffic Control Using Large Language Models: Prompt Engineering, Architecture, and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19299)
*Mahyar Ghazanfari,Matthias Casanova,Jordan Kam,Alex Zongo,Peng Wei,Torsten Darrell,Alexandre Bayen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实验评估了大型语言模型（LLMs）能否生成具有操作真实感的空中交通管制（ATC）通话。研究以旧金山"Bay Tour"航线上的一次通用航空飞行的手工转录文本为基准（P0），设计了五种约束递增的提示结构（P1-P5），嵌入有状态的多轮对话管道中，在九个开源和闭源LLM上系统比较了提示结构、上下文示例及历史条件方式的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ATC通信是安全关键的对话场景，在航空管理其他环节已半自动化的情况下，ATC通话仍高度依赖人类操作。研究旨在探索LLM是否能在此安全关键场景中生成符合操作真实感的通话内容，为LLM辅助ATC奠定基础。

Method: 采用飞行员参与的"人在环路"（pilot-in-the-loop）过程设计五种约束递增的提示结构（P1-P5），并嵌入有状态的多轮对话管道，模型扮演ATC角色应答固定的飞行员转录文本，同时条件化于累积的对话历史。在九个开源/闭源LLM上变化提示、是否提供来自另一实验航班的转录作为上下文示例、以及模型条件是自身先前回复还是注入的真实历史。使用词汇、结构、语义相似度指标及经人类专家标注验证的LLM-as-judge（GPT-5.5）进行评分。

Result: 提供已完成的示例文本能提升相似度，但收紧提示约束反而效果不佳：最轻量的提示表现最好，而脚本化程度最高的提示因其自身错误在对话中累积而崩塌，注入正确的对话历史可以修复这一问题。

Conclusion: 研究勾勒了一条通往LLM辅助ATC的具体路径，同时指出了当前的技术限制，即过多的提示约束可能适得其反，而上下文示例和正确的对话历史注入是提升生成质量的关键因素。

Abstract: Air traffic control (ATC) communication is a safety-critical dialogue that remains largely human-driven even as other parts of air traffic management have been semi-automated. In this article, we experimentally evaluate whether large language models (LLMs) can generate operationally realistic ATC transmissions. An experimental general-aviation flight flying over the San Francisco "Bay Tour" route is hand-transcribed and used as ground truth (P0). Through a pilot-in-the-loop process we design five prompt structures (P1-P5) of increasing constraint and embed them in a stateful multi-turn pipeline, where the model plays ATC to a fixed pilot transcript while conditioning on the accumulating dialogue history. Across nine open- and closed-source LLMs we vary the prompt, the presence of a worked transcript from a different experimental flight as an in-context example, and whether the model conditions on its own prior replies or on injected ground-truth history. Turns are scored with lexical, structural, and semantic similarity metrics and by an LLM-as-judge (GPT-5.5) validated against human expert annotation. Supplying a worked example improves similarity, but tightening the prompt does not: the lightest prompts perform best and the most heavily scripted one collapses as its own errors accumulate through the dialogue, which injecting correct history repairs. These results outline a concrete path and its current limits toward LLM-assisted ATC.

</details>


### [122] [Outcome Monitors: Recovery Affordances for Silent Tool Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19303)
*Sugam Panthi,Rabab Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Outcome Monitors（结果监控器），用于检测智能体工具调用返回结果中违反"结果契约"（outcome contracts）的情况。当工具调用超时时智能体可以感知并绕行，但缓存错误页或负价格等异常结果可能以预期格式到达而被当作事实消费。监控器在检测到违规时保留结果并发出非约束性收据，指明违规属性和公开恢复工具。实验表明该方法在ToolMaze上将完成率从10.9%提升至28.1%，在tau-bench零售场景两个层级分别提升14.0和12.0个百分点，并通过消融实验证明恢复工具列表是有效成分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体系统主要处理工具调用超时等显式失败，但对于以预期格式返回的错误数据（如缓存错误页、负价格）缺乏检测能力，这些"静默失败"会被智能体当作事实消费，导致错误的决策和下游行为。作者希望解决这种格式正确但语义违法的结果无法被识别的问题。

Method: 提出Outcome Monitors：从任务非重叠（task-disjoint）的trace中挖掘或从公开schema推导"结果契约"（对合法输出属性的约束）；监控器在运行时检测结果是否违反契约；一旦违反，保留结果的同时发出非约束性收据（nonbinding receipt），收据包含被违反的属性名称以及公开恢复工具列表。评估采用冻结的、预设定的注入故障场景。

Result: 在ToolMaze基准上，四种模型（两个提供商家族）完成率从10.9%提升到28.1%，并在第三个提供商家族复现。在tau-bench零售场景，两个层级的完成率分别提升14.0和12.0个百分点。消融实验显示：移除恢复工具列表后增益消失，恢复该列表后效果恢复；诊断细节和时序无显著差异。增益集中在故障阻断完成的情形。在基于公开事件分类法转录的测试集上，超出挖掘词表的检测率降至46%，但交付仍继续且完成率不变。

Conclusion: Outcome Monitors能够有效检测格式正确但语义违法的工具结果，并通过恢复工具列表引导智能体绕行故障，显著提升任务完成率。收据中的恢复工具列表是被证明的有效成分，而诊断细节和时序并非关键。当前局限在于超出契约词表的检测率较低，扩展检测能力到契约词表之外仍是开放问题。

Abstract: When a tool call times out, the agent sees the failure and can route around it. A cached error page or negative price can instead arrive in the expected format and be consumed as fact. We introduce Outcome Monitors, which detect violations of outcome contracts mined from task-disjoint traces or derived from public schemas. On a violation, the monitor preserves the result and issues a nonbinding receipt naming the violated property and public recovery tools. In frozen, prespecified evaluations with injected failures, Outcome Monitors raise ToolMaze completion from 10.9% to 28.1% across four models in two provider families and replicate in a third. In tau-bench retail, completion improves by 14.0 and 12.0 points on two tiers. In separate ToolMaze controls, removing the recovery-tool list eliminates the measured gain and restoring it recovers the effect; diagnostic detail and timing produce no detectable differences. Gains concentrate where the fault blocks completion. On a suite transcribed from a published incident taxonomy, detection outside the mined vocabulary falls to 46%, though delivery continues and completion is unchanged. Recovery tools are the active receipt content in these controls; extending detection beyond the contract vocabulary remains open.

</details>


### [123] [Beyond Imitation: Filtering On-Policy Distillation by Reasoning Progress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.19408)
*Chen Yang,Haiyuan Wan,Rengrong Xiong,Yize Chen,Danny H. K. Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出R2-OPD方法，通过构建教师奖励与独立推理进展奖励的双重轨迹内排序，在二者不一致时抑制蒸馏奖励，解决教师反馈与推理进展冲突的问题，提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的On-policy蒸馏假设教师奖励能恰当反映推理进展，但实践中教师奖励常与真实推理进展冲突——推理有实质进步的步骤可能因偏离教师输出而获得较低奖励。这种不一致会误导策略优化。

Method: 提出Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering方法。构建同一轨迹内两组推理跨度（reasoning spans）排序：一组来自教师奖励，另一组来自独立估计的进展奖励。当两组排序不一致时，选择性地抑制蒸馏奖励，以减少与推理进展冲突的监督信号，同时保留有效的教师指导。

Result: 与标准OPD相比，该方法在推理表现上展现出持续一致的改进。

Conclusion: 通过感知推理进展的奖励过滤机制，R2-OPD有效修正了教师奖励与推理进展之间的错配，在保留教师指导价值的同时提升了推理性能。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) has emerged as an effective framework for post-training language models by pairing student-generated trajectories with dense token-level supervision from a teacher. However, OPD implicitly assumes that teacher-derived rewards are an appropriate proxy for reasoning progress, and therefore treats all teacher feedback equally during policy optimization. While in practice, this assumption does not always hold. We observe that teacher-derived rewards often conflict with genuine reasoning progress, as reasoning steps with clear reasoning advancement may still receive lower distillation rewards, simply due to deviation from teacher's outputs. To address this mismatch, we propose Reasoning-Progress-Aware Reward Filtering for On-Policy Distillation (R2-OPD), which constructs two within-trajectory rankings of reasoning spans, one from teacher-derived rewards and the other from independently estimated progress reward.
